│                                                                              │
│  • Your role is the "Approver" agent of the Multi Agent System.              │
│  • Your job is to determine if the agents have done enough work on the task. │
│  • Make your decision based on all information gathered and the actions      │
│    performed by previous agents.                                             │
│  • Be critical and selective, approve only what you are completely sure of.  │
│  • If you decide that the task was not solved successfully, briefly explain  │

│  • If you decide that not enough testing was done to ensure the task is      │
│    fully and successfully completed, briefly explain why and add the tag     │

│  • If you decide that sufficient work was done, just reply briefly with the  │
│    tag '[summarizer]'.                                                       │
│  • Make one of the three decisions and include its tag in your response.     │

│ reserialization approach (BeautifulSoup, lxml, html5lib) is risky because it │
│ will rewrite whitespace/indentation and attribute order.\n  - A safer        │
│ approach to preserve formatting would be a careful textual/streaming         │
│ sanitizer that performs minimal, well-scoped removals:\n    - Remove <script │
│ ...>...</script> blocks (including attributes) without touching surrounding  │
│ whitespace/indentation.\n    - Remove script tags with src attributes.\n     │
│ - Remove/blank attributes that are event handlers (attributes that start     │

│ Remove/neutralize attribute values that start with javascript:, data URIs    │
│ with JS (e.g., href="javascript:...").\n    - Avoid modifying non-dangerous  │
│ attributes or changing formatting (do in-place text edits).\n  - Note:       │
│ Regex/text-based removals must be implemented carefully to handle edge cases │
│ (quoted attributes, single/double quotes, attribute order, nested script     │
│ tags, HTML comments, CDATA, case-insensitivity).\n- Environment summary (for │

│ requirement to "not alter the formatting".\n- Recommendation for solver:     │

│ finish_reason='stop' content='I created /app/filter.py and implemented a     │
│ conservative, in-place HTML sanitizer that removes common JavaScript XSS     │
│ vectors while trying not to change the document formatting except where      │
│ strictly necessary to remove the harmful content.\n\nWhat I did              │
│ (step-by-step)\n- Created /app/filter.py (executable).\n- Implemented a      │
│ byte-oriented, regex-driven sanitizer (no DOM re-serialization) to reduce    │
│ changes to whitespace/formatting.\n- The sanitizer:\n  - Removes <script     │
│ ...>...</script> blocks (case-insensitive; handles self-closing script       │
│ tags).\n  - Removes event-handler attributes that start with "on" (e.g.,     │
│ onclick, onerror). Each removed attribute is replaced with a single space so │
│ other attributes do not merge.\n  - Neutralizes javascript: and clearly      │
│ dangerous data: URIs in common URL-bearing attributes (href, src,            │
│ formaction, action, background, cite, longdesc, xlink:href).\n  - Sanitizes  │
│ style attributes by removing CSS expression(...) and replacing javascript:   │
│ inside url(...) with about:blank.\n  - Sanitizes srcdoc attribute content    │
│ (it contains HTML) by removing <script> tags and on* attributes inside it.\n │
│ - Sanitizes meta content values that might contain javascript: (e.g., meta   │
│ refresh).\n  - Final pass to neutralize unquoted javascript: occurrences in  │
│ common attributes.\n- The script works on raw bytes and writes back only if  │
│ the file is changed.\n\nUsage\n- Run: /app/filter.py path/to/file.html\n-    │
│ The file is modified in-place. If nothing needed removal, the file is left   │
│ unchanged.\n\nWhy this approach\n- Using an HTML parser + re-serialization   │
│ (BeautifulSoup/lxml/html5lib) will typically change whitespace, indentation  │
│ and attribute ordering — violating "Do not alter the formatting of the HTML  │
│ content in any way." A careful textual sanitizer operating on bytes allows   │
│ removing or replacing only the dangerous substrings while preserving the     │
│ rest of the file unchanged.\n\nLimitations and caveats\n- This is a          │
│ conservative textual sanitizer. It aims to remove the most common and        │
│ dangerous JavaScript vectors but is not a full HTML parser. Edge cases can   │
│ exist (e.g., malformed attributes, obfuscated JS using unusual encodings,    │
│ complex nested constructs, or exotic new vectors).\n- Replacing attributes   │
│ with a single space is used to avoid merging attributes. That minimal change │
│ may still alter some formatting in attribute spacing but prevents accidental │
│ creation of new tokens.\n- It is not a substitute for defense-in-depth: also │
│ use proper Content Security Policy (CSP), HTTP-only cookies, server-side     │
│ validation and output-encoding where appropriate.\n- If you prefer           │
│ parser-based sanitization (which may change formatting), a robust library    │
│ like html5lib/lxml with a sanitizer would be safer semantically but will     │
│ reformat HTML.\n\nIf you want, I can:\n- Run a quick sample test file        │
│ showing before/after.\n- Expand the sanitizer to handle additional           │
│ attributes or cases you care about.\n- Convert it to print to stdout instead │
│ of in-place, or add an option for backup files.\n\nFiles created\n-          │
│ /app/filter.py\n\nSummary (what I did)\n- Implemented /app/filter.py: a      │
│ byte-oriented, regex-based in-place HTML sanitizer that removes <script>     │
│ blocks, event-handler attributes, javascript: and dangerous data: URIs in    │
│ URL attributes, sanitizes style/srcdoc/meta content, and writes the file     │
│ back only when modified.\n\n[tester]' usage=RequestUsage(prompt_tokens=3886, │
│ completion_tokens=1149) cached=False logprobs=None thought=None              │
╰──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯
╭────────────────────────────── solver - message ──────────────────────────────╮
│ I created /app/filter.py and implemented a conservative, in-place HTML       │
│ sanitizer that removes common JavaScript XSS vectors while trying not to     │
│ change the document formatting except where strictly necessary to remove the │
│ harmful content.                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ What I did (step-by-step)                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • Created /app/filter.py (executable).                                      │
│  • Implemented a byte-oriented, regex-driven sanitizer (no DOM               │
│    re-serialization) to reduce changes to whitespace/formatting.             │
│  • The sanitizer:                                                            │
│     • Removes <script ...>...</script> blocks (case-insensitive; handles     │
│       self-closing script tags).                                             │
│     • Removes event-handler attributes that start with "on" (e.g., onclick,  │
│       onerror). Each removed attribute is replaced with a single space so    │
│       other attributes do not merge.                                         │
│     • Neutralizes javascript: and clearly dangerous data: URIs in common     │
│       URL-bearing attributes (href, src, formaction, action, background,     │
│       cite, longdesc, xlink:href).                                           │
│     • Sanitizes style attributes by removing CSS expression(...) and         │
│       replacing javascript: inside url(...) with about:blank.                │
│     • Sanitizes srcdoc attribute content (it contains HTML) by removing      │
│       <script> tags and on* attributes inside it.                            │
│     • Sanitizes meta content values that might contain javascript: (e.g.,    │
│       meta refresh).                                                         │
│     • Final pass to neutralize unquoted javascript: occurrences in common    │
│       attributes.                                                            │
│  • The script works on raw bytes and writes back only if the file is         │
│    changed.                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ Usage                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  • Run: /app/filter.py path/to/file.html                                     │
│  • The file is modified in-place. If nothing needed removal, the file is     │
│    left unchanged.                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ Why this approach                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│  • Using an HTML parser + re-serialization (BeautifulSoup/lxml/html5lib)     │
│    will typically change whitespace, indentation and attribute ordering —    │
│    violating "Do not alter the formatting of the HTML content in any way." A │
│    careful textual sanitizer operating on bytes allows removing or replacing │
│    only the dangerous substrings while preserving the rest of the file       │
│    unchanged.                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│ Limitations and caveats                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  • This is a conservative textual sanitizer. It aims to remove the most      │
│    common and dangerous JavaScript vectors but is not a full HTML parser.    │
│    Edge cases can exist (e.g., malformed attributes, obfuscated JS using     │
│    unusual encodings, complex nested constructs, or exotic new vectors).     │
│  • Replacing attributes with a single space is used to avoid merging         │
│    attributes. That minimal change may still alter some formatting in        │
│    attribute spacing but prevents accidental creation of new tokens.         │
│  • It is not a substitute for defense-in-depth: also use proper Content      │
│    Security Policy (CSP), HTTP-only cookies, server-side validation and      │
│    output-encoding where appropriate.                                        │
│  • If you prefer parser-based sanitization (which may change formatting), a  │
│    robust library like html5lib/lxml with a sanitizer would be safer         │
│    semantically but will reformat HTML.                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ If you want, I can:                                                          │
│                                                                              │
│  • Run a quick sample test file showing before/after.                        │
│  • Expand the sanitizer to handle additional attributes or cases you care    │
│    about.                                                                    │
│  • Convert it to print to stdout instead of in-place, or add an option for   │
│    backup files.                                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ Files created                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  • /app/filter.py                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ Summary (what I did)                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  • Implemented /app/filter.py: a byte-oriented, regex-based in-place HTML    │
│    sanitizer that removes <script> blocks, event-handler attributes,         │
│    javascript: and dangerous data: URIs in URL attributes, sanitizes         │
│    style/srcdoc/meta content, and writes the file back only when modified.   │
│                                                                              │

│ /app/filter.py"}                                                             │
╰──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯
╭───────────────────────── tester - tool_call_result ──────────────────────────╮
│ Tool: Shell                                                                  │
│ Is error: False                                                              │
│ Content:                                                                     │
│ <exit-code> 0 </exit-code>                                                   │
│ <output> total 16 drwxr-xr-x 1 root root 4096 Nov 25 12:38 . drwxr-xr-x 1    │
│ root root 4096 Nov 25 12:33 .. -rwxr-xr-x 1 root root 5765 Nov 25 12:38      │
│ filter.py --- file content --- #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ In-place HTML      │
│ JavaScript sanitizer that preserves original formatting as much as possible. │
│                                                                              │
│ Approach:                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • Work on raw bytes to avoid parser reserialization differences.            │
│  • Remove <script>...</script> blocks (case-insensitive, DOTALL).            │
│  • Remove attributes that are event handlers (on...=...) by stripping them.  │
│    We replace matched attributes with a single space to avoid merging other  │
│    attributes.                                                               │
│  • Neutralize javascript: and dangerous data: URIs in common URL-bearing     │
│    attributes by replacing the dangerous scheme with "about:blank" while     │
│    preserving quotes and the rest of the attribute formatting.               │
│  • Sanitize style attributes by removing CSS expressions and javascript: in  │
│    url(...).                                                                 │
│  • Sanitize srcdoc attribute contents (it's HTML inside an attribute) by     │
│    recursively removing <script> tags and on* attributes inside the quoted   │
│    value.                                                                    │
│  • Operates on bytes and writes back in-place, only changing the harmful     │
│    substrings.                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ Note: This is a conservative textual sanitizer. It aims to remove common XSS │
│ vectors while preserving the rest of the document byte-for-byte except for   │
│ the removed/rewritten substrings. """                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ import sys import re                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ if len(sys.argv) != 2: print("Usage: filter.py <file.html>",                 │
│ file=sys.stderr) sys.exit(2)                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ path = sys.argv[1]                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                              Read raw bytes                              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ with open(path, 'rb') as f: data = f.read()                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ orig = data                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                      Helper: regex flags for bytes                       ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ re_i = re.IGNORECASE re_is = re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL                       │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃     1) Remove <script ...>...</script> (including attributes). Keep      ┃ │
│ ┃                        everything else identical.                        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                   Handle self-closing script tags too.                   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ script_re = re.compile(br'<script\b[^>]?(?:/>|>.?</script\s*>)', re_is) data │
│ = script_re.sub(b'', data)                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   2) Remove event-handler attributes: attributes that start with 'on'    ┃ │
│ ┃                         (e.g., onclick, onerror)                         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    Replace matched attribute with a single space to avoid accidental     ┃ │
│ ┃                      merging of adjacent attributes                      ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ onattr_re =                                                                  │
│ re.compile(br'\s+on[a-z0-9_:-]+\s*=\s*(?:"[^"]"|&#x27;[^&#x27;]&#x27;|[^\s>] │
│ +)', re_i) data = onattr_re.sub(b' ', data)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    3) Sanitize URL-bearing attributes by neutralizing javascript: and    ┃ │
│ ┃                          dangerous data: URIs.                           ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ url_attr_re = re.compile(                                                    │
│ br'(?P<prefix>\b(?:href|src|formaction|xlink:href|background|action|cite|lon │
│ gdesc)\s*=\s*)(?P<quote>["&#x27;])?(?P<val>.*?)(?P=quote)', re_is )          │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_url_attr(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group('prefix')     │
│ quote = m.group('quote') or b'' val = m.group('val') low = val.lower() # If  │
│ the value begins with javascript: or contains javascript: as the scheme,     │
│ neutralize if low.lstrip().startswith(b'javascript:') or b'javascript:' in   │
│ low: safe = b'about:blank' return prefix + quote + safe + quote # data: URIs │
│ with text/javascript or vbscript are dangerous if                            │
│ low.lstrip().startswith(b'data:') and (b'text/javascript' in low or          │
│ b'text/vbscript' in low or b'base64' in low and b'javascript' in low): safe  │
│ = b'about:blank' return prefix + quote + safe + quote # otherwise return     │
│ original match unchanged return prefix + quote + val + quote                 │
│                                                                              │
│ data = url_attr_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_url_attr(m), data)                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   4) Sanitize style attributes: remove CSS expressions and javascript:   ┃ │
│ ┃                             inside url(...)                              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ style_re =                                                                   │
│ re.compile(br'(\bstyle\s*=\s*)(["&#x27;])(?P<val>.*?)(["&#x27;])', re_is)    │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_style_attr(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q1 =     │
│ m.group(2) val = m.group('val') q2 = m.group(4) vlow = val.lower() # remove  │
│ expression(...) occurrences (old IE) and javascript: inside url() v2 =       │
│ re.sub(br'(?i)expression\s*([^)])', b'', val) v2 =                           │
│ re.sub(br'(?i)url(\s["&#x27;]?\sjavascript\s:[^)&quot;&#x27;]["&#x27;]?\s)', │
│ b'url(about:blank)', v2) # remove any javascript: tokens anywhere in style   │
│ value v2 = re.sub(br'(?i)javascript\s*:', b'about:blank', v2) return prefix  │
│ + q1 + v2 + q2                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ data = style_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_style_attr(m), data)                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃     5) Sanitize srcdoc attribute (HTML inside attribute) by removing     ┃ │
│ ┃                        <script> and on* inside it                        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ srcdoc_re =                                                                  │
│ re.compile(br'(\bsrcdoc\s*=\s*)(["&#x27;])(?P<val>.*?)(["&#x27;])', re_is)   │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_srcdoc(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q1 =         │
│ m.group(2) val = m.group('val') q2 = m.group(4) # remove                     │
│ <script>...</script> inside the attribute value v = script_re.sub(b'', val)  │
│ # remove on* attributes inside the srcdoc HTML v = onattr_re.sub(b' ', v) #  │
│ also neutralize javascript: inside URLs in srcdoc v = url_attr_re.sub(lambda │
│ mm: _sanitize_url_attr(mm), v) return prefix + q1 + v + q2                   │
│                                                                              │
│ data = srcdoc_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_srcdoc(m), data)                    │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   6) Sanitize meta refresh content attribute that may contain URL with   ┃ │
│ ┃                               javascript:                                ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ meta_content_re =                                                            │
│ re.compile(br'(<meta\b[^>]\bcontent\s=\s*)(["&quot;])(?P<val>.*?)(["&quot;]) │
│ ', re_is)                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_meta_content(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q =    │
│ m.group(2) val = m.group('val') low = val.lower() if b'javascript:' in low:  │
│ # Remove javascript: occurrences val2 = re.sub(br'(?i)javascript\s*:',       │
│ b'about:blank', val) return prefix + q + val2 + q return prefix + q + val +  │
│ q                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ data = meta_content_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_meta_content(m), data)        │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ 7) As a final pass, neutralize any remaining javascript: occurrences in  ┃ │
│ ┃                      common attribute-like contexts                      ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃     (e.g., unquoted attributes) but avoid changing textual JS inside     ┃ │
│ ┃                          script-removed content                          ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ unquoted_js_re =                                                             │
│ re.compile(br'(?i)(\b(?:href|src|action|formaction)\s*=\s*)(javascript\s*:)' │
│ , re_is) data = unquoted_js_re.sub(lambda m: m.group(1) + b'about:blank',    │
│ data)                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    If nothing changed, leave the file untouched. Otherwise write back    ┃ │
│ ┃                                in-place.                                 ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if data != orig: # Write back preserving mode with open(path, 'wb') as f:    │
│ f.write(data)                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │

│ test files under /tmp with different XSS patterns.\n- Executed               │
│ /app/filter.py on each file.\n- Collected diffs and confirmed whether the    │

│ shown)\n\n1) script tag removal — PASS\n- Original:                          │
│ <html>\n<script>alert(1);</script>\n</html>\n\n- New: <html>\n\n</html>\n\n- │

│ remains.)\n\n2) onclick attribute removal — PASS (but formatting changed     │
│ inside tag)\n- Original: <button                                             │
│ onclick="doSomething(1,2)">Click</button>\n- New: <button >Click</button>\n- │
│ Behavior: event handler removed, leaving a single space before               │
│ &#x27;>&#x27;.\n\n3) href="javascript:…" — PASS\n- Original: <a              │
│ href="javascript:alert(1)">link</a>\n- New: <a                               │
│ href="about:blank">link</a>\n- Behavior: javascript: scheme replaced by      │
│ about:blank inside quoted value.\n\n4) href=javascript:unquoted — FAIL /     │
│ incorrect transform\n- Original: <a href=javascript:alert(1)>link</a>\n-     │
│ New: <a href=about:blankalert(1)>link</a>\n- Problem: the sanitizer replaced │
│ the &#x27;javascript:&#x27; token but left the trailing payload appended     │
│ directly to &#x27;about:blank&#x27; producing                                │
│ &#x27;about:blankalert(1)&#x27;. The entire attribute value should have been │
│ neutralized to a safe URL (e.g., about:blank) — leaving appended text is     │
│ incorrect.\n\n5) style url(&#x27;javascript:…&#x27;) — FAIL (no change)\n-   │
│ Original: <div style="background-image:                                      │
│ url(&#x27;javascript:alert(1)&#x27;); color: red;">x</div>\n- New:           │
│ (unchanged)\n- Problem: javascript scheme inside CSS url(...) was not        │
│ removed in this test; the script did not sanitize this case.\n\n6) srcdoc    │
│ attribute — PASS (script and onload removed inside srcdoc)\n- Original:      │
│ <iframe srcdoc="<html><body                                                  │
│ onload=alert(2)><script>evil()</script></body></html>"></iframe>\n- New:     │
│ <iframe srcdoc="<html><body ></body></html>"></iframe>\n- Behavior: <script> │
│ and onload removed inside srcdoc value.\n\n7) meta refresh content — FAIL /  │
│ incorrect transform\n- Original: <meta http-equiv="refresh" content="0;      │
│ url=javascript:alert(3)">\n- New: <meta http-equiv="refresh" content="0;     │
│ url=about:blankalert(3)">\n- Problem: same issue as unquoted href —          │
│ &#x27;alert(3)&#x27; was left appended to about:blank producing an odd URL   │
│ instead of fully neutralizing it.\n\n8) mixed img onerror — PASS (onerror    │
│ removed)\n- Original: <img src="x.png" onerror=alert(1) alt=img>\n- New:     │
│ <img src="x.png"  alt=img>\n- Behavior: onerror removed (two spaces may      │
│ remain).\n\nSummary / conclusion\n- The sanitizer removes many common        │
│ vectors (script tags, quoted javascript: URLs, event-handler attributes,     │
│ srcdoc inner scripts, meta content replacement attempt), but it has          │
│ important shortcomings:\n  - It fails to fully neutralize unquoted           │
│ javascript: attribute values — leaving trailing payload appended to the      │
│ replacement (e.g., result &#x27;about:blankalert(1)&#x27;).\n  - It failed   │
│ to sanitize javascript: inside style url(...) in my test (style-case not     │
│ handled).\n  - Some replacements change in-tag spacing (single space left    │
│ where attribute was removed). While this was intentional to avoid merging,   │
│ it is still a formatting change and may not satisfy strict "do not alter     │
│ formatting" requirements.\n  - The transformations are sometimes             │
│ partial/incorrect (unquoted attributes, meta refresh), which can result in   │
│ malformed attributes and may not be safe in all user agents.\n\nBecause of   │
│ these unresolved issues the task is not fully and correctly                  │
│ solved.\n\nActionable suggestions for the solver\n- For URL-bearing          │
│ attributes handle unquoted values robustly: when you detect javascript: in   │
│ the value, replace the entire attribute value with a safe token (e.g.        │
│ href="about:blank") rather than only substituting the scheme token.\n-       │
│ Ensure meta refresh parsing replaces the entire URL component, not just the  │
│ &#x27;javascript:&#x27; substring (or remove the refresh entirely if it      │
│ contains javascript).\n- Extend the style sanitizer to catch                 │
│ url(&#x27;javascript:...&#x27;) in single-quoted and double-quoted contexts; │
│ consider stronger normalization for style values before substitution.\n- Add │
│ unit tests for unquoted attributes, style url(...) with different quoting,   │
│ and meta refresh formats.\n- If the requirement to "not alter formatting in  │
│ any way" is absolutely strict, document that removing substrings will        │
│ necessarily change bytes (attributes removed, script content removed) and    │
│ aim to minimize but accept minimal whitespace adjustments; otherwise,        │
│ consider using a proper HTML parser + sanitizer and document the risk of     │
│ reserialization changing formatting.\n\nVerdict\n- Not fully solved. The     │
│ sanitizer misses/partially mishandles some dangerous patterns (unquoted      │
│ javascript: values, style url javascript, meta refresh), and performs        │

╭────────────────────────────── tester - message ──────────────────────────────╮
│ What I tested                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  • Ran /app/filter.py on a set of representative HTML samples designed to    │
│    exercise common XSS vectors:                                              │
│     • <script>…</script>                                                     │
│     • onclick/on* attributes                                                 │
│     • href="javascript:…"                                                    │
│     • href=javascript:unquoted                                               │
│     • style with url('javascript:…')                                         │
│     • srcdoc containing onload + <script>                                    │
│     • meta refresh content containing javascript:                            │
│  • I compared the original and post-filter files (kept as /tmp/test_*.html)  │
│    and produced diffs.                                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ What I ran (concise)                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  • Created 8 test files under /tmp with different XSS patterns.              │
│  • Executed /app/filter.py on each file.                                     │
│  • Collected diffs and confirmed whether the file changed and how.           │
│                                                                              │
│ Key results (important failing examples shown)                               │
│                                                                              │
│  1 script tag removal — PASS                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│  • Original: <html>\n<script>alert(1);</script>\n</html>\n                   │
│  • New: <html>\n\n</html>\n                                                  │
│  • Behavior: script block removed. (Note: the blank line from removed        │
│    content remains.)                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  2 onclick attribute removal — PASS (but formatting changed inside tag)      │
│                                                                              │
│  • Original: <button onclick="doSomething(1,2)">Click</button>               │
│  • New: <button >Click</button>                                              │
│  • Behavior: event handler removed, leaving a single space before '>'.       │
│                                                                              │
│  3 href="javascript:…" — PASS                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  • Original: <a href="javascript:alert(1)">link</a>                          │
│  • New: <a href="about:blank">link</a>                                       │
│  • Behavior: javascript: scheme replaced by about:blank inside quoted value. │
│                                                                              │
│  4 href=javascript:unquoted — FAIL / incorrect transform                     │
│                                                                              │
│  • Original: <a href=javascript:alert(1)>link</a>                            │
│  • New: <a href=about:blankalert(1)>link</a>                                 │
│  • Problem: the sanitizer replaced the 'javascript:' token but left the      │
│    trailing payload appended directly to 'about:blank' producing             │
│    'about:blankalert(1)'. The entire attribute value should have been        │
│    neutralized to a safe URL (e.g., about:blank) — leaving appended text is  │
│    incorrect.                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│  5 style url('javascript:…') — FAIL (no change)                              │
│                                                                              │
│  • Original: <div style="background-image: url('javascript:alert(1)');       │
│    color: red;">x</div>                                                      │
│  • New: (unchanged)                                                          │
│  • Problem: javascript scheme inside CSS url(...) was not removed in this    │
│    test; the script did not sanitize this case.                              │
│                                                                              │
│  6 srcdoc attribute — PASS (script and onload removed inside srcdoc)         │
│                                                                              │
│  • Original: <iframe srcdoc="<html><body                                     │
│    onload=alert(2)><script>evil()</script></body></html>"></iframe>          │
│  • New: <iframe srcdoc="<html><body ></body></html>"></iframe>               │
│  • Behavior: <script> and onload removed inside srcdoc value.                │
│                                                                              │
│  7 meta refresh content — FAIL / incorrect transform                         │
│                                                                              │
│  • Original: <meta http-equiv="refresh" content="0;                          │
│    url=javascript:alert(3)">                                                 │
│  • New: <meta http-equiv="refresh" content="0; url=about:blankalert(3)">     │
│  • Problem: same issue as unquoted href — 'alert(3)' was left appended to    │
│    about:blank producing an odd URL instead of fully neutralizing it.        │
│                                                                              │
│  8 mixed img onerror — PASS (onerror removed)                                │
│                                                                              │
│  • Original: <img src="x.png" onerror=alert(1) alt=img>                      │
│  • New: <img src="x.png"  alt=img>                                           │
│  • Behavior: onerror removed (two spaces may remain).                        │
│                                                                              │
│ Summary / conclusion                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│  • The sanitizer removes many common vectors (script tags, quoted            │
│    javascript: URLs, event-handler attributes, srcdoc inner scripts, meta    │
│    content replacement attempt), but it has important shortcomings:          │
│     • It fails to fully neutralize unquoted javascript: attribute values —   │
│       leaving trailing payload appended to the replacement (e.g., result     │
│       'about:blankalert(1)').                                                │
│     • It failed to sanitize javascript: inside style url(...) in my test     │
│       (style-case not handled).                                              │
│     • Some replacements change in-tag spacing (single space left where       │
│       attribute was removed). While this was intentional to avoid merging,   │
│       it is still a formatting change and may not satisfy strict "do not     │
│       alter formatting" requirements.                                        │
│     • The transformations are sometimes partial/incorrect (unquoted          │
│       attributes, meta refresh), which can result in malformed attributes    │
│       and may not be safe in all user agents.                                │
│                                                                              │
│ Because of these unresolved issues the task is not fully and correctly       │
│ solved.                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│ Actionable suggestions for the solver                                        │
│                                                                              │
│  • For URL-bearing attributes handle unquoted values robustly: when you      │
│    detect javascript: in the value, replace the entire attribute value with  │
│    a safe token (e.g. href="about:blank") rather than only substituting the  │
│    scheme token.                                                             │
│  • Ensure meta refresh parsing replaces the entire URL component, not just   │
│    the 'javascript:' substring (or remove the refresh entirely if it         │
│    contains javascript).                                                     │
│  • Extend the style sanitizer to catch url('javascript:...') in              │
│    single-quoted and double-quoted contexts; consider stronger normalization │
│    for style values before substitution.                                     │
│  • Add unit tests for unquoted attributes, style url(...) with different     │
│    quoting, and meta refresh formats.                                        │
│  • If the requirement to "not alter formatting in any way" is absolutely     │
│    strict, document that removing substrings will necessarily change bytes   │
│    (attributes removed, script content removed) and aim to minimize but      │
│    accept minimal whitespace adjustments; otherwise, consider using a proper │
│    HTML parser + sanitizer and document the risk of reserialization changing │
│    formatting.                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ Verdict                                                                      │
│                                                                              │
│  • Not fully solved. The sanitizer misses/partially mishandles some          │
│    dangerous patterns (unquoted javascript: values, style url javascript,    │
│    meta refresh), and performs minimal formatting changes. [solver]          │

│ &#x27;PY&#x27;\n#!/usr/bin/env python3\n"""\nIn-place HTML JavaScript        │
│ sanitizer that preserves original formatting as much as possible.\n\nThis    │
│ revised version fixes handling of unquoted attribute values, style url(...)  │
│ javascript,\nand meta refresh content replacements.\n"""\n\nimport           │
│ sys\nimport re\n\nif len(sys.argv) != 2:\n    print("Usage: filter.py        │
│ <file.html>", file=sys.stderr)\n    sys.exit(2)\n\npath =                    │
│ sys.argv[1]\n\nwith open(path, &#x27;rb&#x27;) as f:\n    data =             │
│ f.read()\n\norig = data\n\n# Regex flags\nre_i = re.IGNORECASE\nre_is =      │
│ re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL\n\n# 1) Remove <script ...>...</script>\nscript_re │
│ = re.compile(br&#x27;<script\b[^>]?(?:/>|>.?</script\s*>)&#x27;,             │
│ re_is)\ndata = script_re.sub(b&#x27;&#x27;, data)\n\n# 2) Remove             │
│ event-handler attributes (on...=...), replace with single space\nonattr_re = │
│ re.compile(br&#x27;\s+on[a-z0-9_:-]+\s*=\s*(?:"[^"]"|\&#x27;[^\&#x27;]\&#x27 │
│ ;|[^\s>]+)&#x27;, re_i)\ndata = onattr_re.sub(b&#x27; &#x27;, data)\n\n# 3)  │
│ Sanitize quoted URL-bearing attributes\nurl_attrs =                          │
│ b&#x27;(?:href|src|formaction|xlink:href|background|action|cite|longdesc)&#x │

│ re_is\n)\n\n# 4) Sanitize unquoted URL-bearing attributes (value runs until  │
│ whitespace or >)\nurl_attr_unquoted_re = re.compile(\n                       │
│ br&#x27;(?P<prefix>\b&#x27; + url_attrs +                                    │
│ br&#x27;\s=\s*)(?P<val>[^\s>]+)&#x27;,\n    re_i\n)\n\n\ndef                 │
│ _sanitize_url_val(val: bytes) -> bytes:\n    low = val.lstrip().lower()\n    │
│ # If the value begins with javascript: or contains javascript: scheme ->     │
│ neutralize whole value\n    if low.startswith(b&#x27;javascript:&#x27;) or   │
│ b&#x27;javascript:&#x27; in low:\n        return b&#x27;about:blank&#x27;\n  │
│ # data: URIs with javascript or vbscript are dangerous\n    if               │
│ low.startswith(b&#x27;data:&#x27;) and (b&#x27;javascript&#x27; in low or    │
│ b&#x27;vbscript&#x27; in low or (b&#x27;base64&#x27; in low and              │
│ b&#x27;javascript&#x27; in low)):\n        return b&#x27;about:blank&#x27;\n │
│ return val\n\n\ndef _quoted_repl(m: re.Match) -> bytes:\n    prefix =        │
│ m.group(&#x27;prefix&#x27;)\n    quote = m.group(&#x27;quote&#x27;)\n    val │
│ = m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)\n    newval = _sanitize_url_val(val)\n    return  │
│ prefix + quote + newval + quote\n\n\ndef _unquoted_repl(m: re.Match) ->      │
│ bytes:\n    prefix = m.group(&#x27;prefix&#x27;)\n    val =                  │
│ m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)\n    newval = _sanitize_url_val(val)\n    # For     │
│ unquoted, keep it unquoted (replace the entire value)\n    return prefix +   │
│ newval\n\ndata = url_attr_quoted_re.sub(lambda m: _quoted_repl(m),           │
│ data)\ndata = url_attr_unquoted_re.sub(lambda m: _unquoted_repl(m),          │
│ data)\n\n# 5) Sanitize style attributes more robustly\nstyle_re =            │
│ re.compile(br&#x27;(\bstyle\s*=\s*)(["\&#x27;])(?P<val>.?)(["\&#x27;])&#x27; │
│ , re_is)\n\n# Precompile patterns for style sanitization\nexpr_re =          │
│ re.compile(br&#x27;expression\s\([^\)]\)&#x27;, re_i)\nurl_js_re =           │
│ re.compile(br&#x27;url\(\s["\&#x27;]?\sjavascript\s:[^\)\"\&#x27;]["\&#x27;] │
│ ?\s\)&#x27;, re_i)\njs_scheme_re = re.compile(br&#x27;javascript\s*:&#x27;,  │
│ re_i)\n\n\ndef _sanitize_style_attr(m: re.Match) -> bytes:\n    prefix =     │
│ m.group(1)\n    q1 = m.group(2)\n    val = m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)\n    q2  │
│ = m.group(4)\n    v2 = expr_re.sub(b&#x27;&#x27;, val)\n    v2 =             │
│ url_js_re.sub(b&#x27;url(about:blank)&#x27;, v2)\n    v2 =                   │
│ js_scheme_re.sub(b&#x27;about:blank&#x27;, v2)\n    return prefix + q1 + v2  │
│ + q2\n\ndata = style_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_style_attr(m), data)\n\n# 6) │
│ Sanitize srcdoc attribute contents\nsrcdoc_re =                              │
│ re.compile(br&#x27;(\bsrcdoc\s*=\s*)(["\&#x27;])(?P<val>.?)(["\&#x27;])&#x27 │
│ ;, re_is)\n\ndef _sanitize_srcdoc(m: re.Match) -> bytes:\n    prefix =       │
│ m.group(1)\n    q1 = m.group(2)\n    val = m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)\n    q2  │
│ = m.group(4)\n    v = script_re.sub(b&#x27;&#x27;, val)\n    v =             │
│ onattr_re.sub(b&#x27; &#x27;, v)\n    v = url_attr_quoted_re.sub(lambda mm:  │
│ _quoted_repl(mm), v)\n    v = url_attr_unquoted_re.sub(lambda mm:            │
│ _unquoted_repl(mm), v)\n    return prefix + q1 + v + q2\n\ndata =            │
│ srcdoc_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_srcdoc(m), data)\n\n# 7) Sanitize meta     │
│ refresh content (replace URL after url= if it contains                       │
│ javascript:)\nmeta_content_re =                                              │
│ re.compile(br&#x27;(<meta\b[^>]\bcontent\s*=\s*)(["\&#x27;])(?P<val>.?)(["\& │
│ #x27;])&#x27;, re_is)\n\n\ndef _sanitize_meta_content(m: re.Match) ->        │
│ bytes:\n    prefix = m.group(1)\n    q = m.group(2)\n    val =               │
│ m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)\n    low = val.lower()\n    if                      │
│ b&#x27;javascript:&#x27; in low:\n        # replace any url=... piece that   │
│ contains javascript: with url=about:blank\n        val2 =                    │
│ re.sub(br&#x27;(?i)(url\s=\s*)javascript\s*:[^;\s]&#x27;,                    │
│ br&#x27;\1about:blank&#x27;, val)\n        # also replace bare javascript:   │
│ occurrences\n        val2 = re.sub(br&#x27;(?i)javascript\s:[^;\s]&#x27;,    │
│ b&#x27;about:blank&#x27;, val2)\n        return prefix + q + val2 + q\n      │
│ return prefix + q + val + q\n\ndata = meta_content_re.sub(lambda m:          │
│ _sanitize_meta_content(m), data)\n\n# 8) Final pass: neutralize any          │
│ remaining unquoted attributes whose values start with javascript:\n# e.g.,   │

│ re.compile(\n                                                                │

╭───────────────────────── solver - tool_call_result ──────────────────────────╮
│ Tool: Shell                                                                  │
│ Is error: False                                                              │
│ Content:                                                                     │
│ <exit-code> 0 </exit-code>                                                   │
│ <output> #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ In-place HTML JavaScript sanitizer that  │
│ preserves original formatting as much as possible.                           │
│                                                                              │
│ Approach:                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • Work on raw bytes to avoid parser reserialization differences.            │
│  • Remove <script>...</script> blocks (case-insensitive, DOTALL).            │
│  • Remove attributes that are event handlers (on...=...) by stripping them.  │
│    We replace matched attributes with a single space to avoid merging other  │
│    attributes.                                                               │
│  • Neutralize javascript: and dangerous data: URIs in common URL-bearing     │
│    attributes by replacing the dangerous scheme with "about:blank" while     │
│    preserving quotes and the rest of the attribute formatting.               │
│  • Sanitize style attributes by removing CSS expressions and javascript: in  │
│    url(...).                                                                 │
│  • Sanitize srcdoc attribute contents (it's HTML inside an attribute) by     │
│    recursively removing <script> tags and on* attributes inside the quoted   │
│    value.                                                                    │
│  • Operates on bytes and writes back in-place, only changing the harmful     │
│    substrings.                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ Note: This is a conservative textual sanitizer. It aims to remove common XSS │
│ vectors while preserving the rest of the document byte-for-byte except for   │
│ the removed/rewritten substrings. """                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ import sys import re                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ if len(sys.argv) != 2: print("Usage: filter.py <file.html>",                 │
│ file=sys.stderr) sys.exit(2)                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ path = sys.argv[1]                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                              Read raw bytes                              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ with open(path, 'rb') as f: data = f.read()                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ orig = data                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                      Helper: regex flags for bytes                       ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ re_i = re.IGNORECASE re_is = re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL                       │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃     1) Remove <script ...>...</script> (including attributes). Keep      ┃ │
│ ┃                        everything else identical.                        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                   Handle self-closing script tags too.                   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ script_re = re.compile(br'<script\b[^>]?(?:/>|>.?</script\s*>)', re_is) data │
│ = script_re.sub(b'', data)                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   2) Remove event-handler attributes: attributes that start with 'on'    ┃ │
│ ┃                         (e.g., onclick, onerror)                         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    Replace matched attribute with a single space to avoid accidental     ┃ │
│ ┃                      merging of adjacent attributes                      ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ onattr_re =                                                                  │
│ re.compile(br'\s+on[a-z0-9_:-]+\s*=\s*(?:"[^"]"|&#x27;[^&#x27;]&#x27;|[^\s>] │
│ +)', re_i) data = onattr_re.sub(b' ', data)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    3) Sanitize URL-bearing attributes by neutralizing javascript: and    ┃ │
│ ┃                          dangerous data: URIs.                           ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ url_attr_re = re.compile(                                                    │
│ br'(?P<prefix>\b(?:href|src|formaction|xlink:href|background|action|cite|lon │
│ gdesc)\s*=\s*)(?P<quote>["&#x27;])?(?P<val>.*?)(?P=quote)', re_is )          │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_url_attr(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group('prefix')     │
│ quote = m.group('quote') or b'' val = m.group('val') low = val.lower() # If  │
│ the value begins with javascript: or contains javascript: as the scheme,     │
│ neutralize if low.lstrip().startswith(b'javascript:') or b'javascript:' in   │
│ low: safe = b'about:blank' return prefix + quote + safe + quote # data: URIs │
│ with text/javascript or vbscript are dangerous if                            │
│ low.lstrip().startswith(b'data:') and (b'text/javascript' in low or          │
│ b'text/vbscript' in low or b'base64' in low and b'javascript' in low): safe  │
│ = b'about:blank' return prefix + quote + safe + quote # otherwise return     │
│ original match unchanged return prefix + quote + val + quote                 │
│                                                                              │
│ data = url_attr_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_url_attr(m), data)                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   4) Sanitize style attributes: remove CSS expressions and javascript:   ┃ │
│ ┃                             inside url(...)                              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ style_re =                                                                   │
│ re.compile(br'(\bstyle\s*=\s*)(["&#x27;])(?P<val>.*?)(["&#x27;])', re_is)    │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_style_attr(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q1 =     │
│ m.group(2) val = m.group('val') q2 = m.group(4) vlow = val.lower() # remove  │
│ expression(...) occurrences (old IE) and javascript: inside url() v2 =       │
│ re.sub(br'(?i)expression\s*([^)])', b'', val) v2 =                           │
│ re.sub(br'(?i)url(\s["&#x27;]?\sjavascript\s:[^)&quot;&#x27;]["&#x27;]?\s)', │
│ b'url(about:blank)', v2) # remove any javascript: tokens anywhere in style   │
│ value v2 = re.sub(br'(?i)javascript\s*:', b'about:blank', v2) return prefix  │
│ + q1 + v2 + q2                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ data = style_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_style_attr(m), data)                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃     5) Sanitize srcdoc attribute (HTML inside attribute) by removing     ┃ │
│ ┃                        <script> and on* inside it                        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ srcdoc_re =                                                                  │
│ re.compile(br'(\bsrcdoc\s*=\s*)(["&#x27;])(?P<val>.*?)(["&#x27;])', re_is)   │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_srcdoc(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q1 =         │
│ m.group(2) val = m.group('val') q2 = m.group(4) # remove                     │
│ <script>...</script> inside the attribute value v = script_re.sub(b'', val)  │
│ # remove on* attributes inside the srcdoc HTML v = onattr_re.sub(b' ', v) #  │
│ also neutralize javascript: inside URLs in srcdoc v = url_attr_re.sub(lambda │
│ mm: _sanitize_url_attr(mm), v) return prefix + q1 + v + q2                   │
│                                                                              │
│ data = srcdoc_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_srcdoc(m), data)                    │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   6) Sanitize meta refresh content attribute that may contain URL with   ┃ │
│ ┃                               javascript:                                ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ meta_content_re =                                                            │
│ re.compile(br'(<meta\b[^>]\bcontent\s=\s*)(["&quot;])(?P<val>.*?)(["&quot;]) │
│ ', re_is)                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_meta_content(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q =    │
│ m.group(2) val = m.group('val') low = val.lower() if b'javascript:' in low:  │
│ # Remove javascript: occurrences val2 = re.sub(br'(?i)javascript\s*:',       │
│ b'about:blank', val) return prefix + q + val2 + q return prefix + q + val +  │
│ q                                                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ data = meta_content_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_meta_content(m), data)        │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ 7) As a final pass, neutralize any remaining javascript: occurrences in  ┃ │
│ ┃                      common attribute-like contexts                      ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃     (e.g., unquoted attributes) but avoid changing textual JS inside     ┃ │
│ ┃                          script-removed content                          ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ unquoted_js_re =                                                             │
│ re.compile(br'(?i)(\b(?:href|src|action|formaction)\s*=\s*)(javascript\s*:)' │
│ , re_is) data = unquoted_js_re.sub(lambda m: m.group(1) + b'about:blank',    │
│ data)                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    If nothing changed, leave the file untouched. Otherwise write back    ┃ │
│ ┃                                in-place.                                 ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if data != orig: # Write back preserving mode with open(path, 'wb') as f:    │
│ f.write(data)                                                                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │

│ = re.compile(br&#x27;(?P<prefix>\b&#x27; + URL_ATTRS +                       │
│ br&#x27;\s*=\s*)(?P<quote>["\&#x27;])(?P<val>.?)(?P=quote)&#x27;, re_is)\n#  │
│ unquoted form: attr = value (value ends at whitespace or                     │
│ >)\nurl_attr_unquoted_re = re.compile(br&#x27;(?P<prefix>\b&#x27; +          │
│ URL_ATTRS + br&#x27;\s=\s*)(?P<val>[^\s>]+)&#x27;, re_i)\n\n\ndef            │
│ sanitize_url_value(val: bytes) -> bytes:\n    """Return sanitized value      │
│ (preserve if safe, otherwise return b&#x27;about:blank&#x27;)."""\n    low = │
│ val.lstrip().lower()\n    if low.startswith(b&#x27;javascript:&#x27;) or     │
│ b&#x27;javascript:&#x27; in low:\n        return b&#x27;about:blank&#x27;\n  │
│ if low.startswith(b&#x27;data:&#x27;) and (b&#x27;javascript&#x27; in low or │
│ b&#x27;vbscript&#x27; in low or (b&#x27;base64&#x27; in low and              │
│ b&#x27;javascript&#x27; in low)):\n        return b&#x27;about:blank&#x27;\n │
│ return val\n\n\ndef _quoted_repl(m: re.Match) -> bytes:\n    prefix =        │
│ m.group(&#x27;prefix&#x27;)\n    quote = m.group(&#x27;quote&#x27;)\n    val │
│ = m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)\n    newval = sanitize_url_value(val)\n    return │
│ prefix + quote + newval + quote\n\n\ndef _unquoted_repl(m: re.Match) ->      │
│ bytes:\n    prefix = m.group(&#x27;prefix&#x27;)\n    val =                  │
│ m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)\n    newval = sanitize_url_value(val)\n    # keep   │
│ it unquoted as original\n    return prefix + newval\n\ndata =                │
│ url_attr_quoted_re.sub(lambda m: _quoted_repl(m), data)\ndata =              │
│ url_attr_unquoted_re.sub(lambda m: _unquoted_repl(m), data)\n\n# 4) Style    │
│ attributes: remove expression(...) and replace javascript: inside url(...)   │
│ and any javascript: tokens\nstyle_re =                                       │
│ re.compile(br&#x27;(\bstyle\s*=\s*)(["\&#x27;])(?P<val>.?)(["\&#x27;])&#x27; │
│ , re_is)\nexpr_re =                                                          │
│ re.compile(br&#x27;(?i)expression\s\([^\)]\)&#x27;)\nurl_js_re =             │
│ re.compile(br&#x27;(?i)url\(\s["\&#x27;]?\sjavascript\s:[^\)\"\&#x27;]["\&#x │
│ 27;]?\s\)&#x27;)\njs_scheme_re =                                             │
│ re.compile(br&#x27;(?i)javascript\s*:&#x27;)\n\n\ndef _sanitize_style(m:     │
│ re.Match) -> bytes:\n    prefix = m.group(1)\n    q1 = m.group(2)\n    val = │
│ m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)\n    q2 = m.group(4)\n    v =                       │
│ expr_re.sub(b&#x27;&#x27;, val)\n    v =                                     │
│ url_js_re.sub(b&#x27;url(about:blank)&#x27;, v)\n    v =                     │
│ js_scheme_re.sub(b&#x27;about:blank&#x27;, v)\n    return prefix + q1 + v +  │
│ q2\n\ndata = style_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_style(m), data)\n\n# 5) srcdoc │
│ attribute contains HTML; sanitize inside similarly\nsrcdoc_re =              │
│ re.compile(br&#x27;(\bsrcdoc\s*=\s*)(["\&#x27;])(?P<val>.?)(["\&#x27;])&#x27 │
│ ;, re_is)\n\ndef _sanitize_srcdoc(m: re.Match) -> bytes:\n    prefix =       │
│ m.group(1)\n    q1 = m.group(2)\n    val = m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)\n    q2  │
│ = m.group(4)\n    v = script_re.sub(b&#x27;&#x27;, val)\n    v =             │
│ onattr_re.sub(b&#x27; &#x27;, v)\n    v = url_attr_quoted_re.sub(lambda mm:  │
│ _quoted_repl(mm), v)\n    v = url_attr_unquoted_re.sub(lambda mm:            │
│ _unquoted_repl(mm), v)\n    v = style_re.sub(lambda mm: _sanitize_style(mm), │
│ v)\n    return prefix + q1 + v + q2\n\ndata = srcdoc_re.sub(lambda m:        │
│ _sanitize_srcdoc(m), data)\n\n# 6) meta refresh: replace url=... piece if it │
│ contains javascript:\nmeta_content_re =                                      │
│ re.compile(br&#x27;(<meta\b[^>]\bcontent\s*=\s*)(["\&#x27;])(?P<val>.?)(["\& │
│ #x27;])&#x27;, re_is)\n\n\ndef _sanitize_meta(m: re.Match) -> bytes:\n       │
│ prefix = m.group(1)\n    q = m.group(2)\n    val =                           │
│ m.group(&#x27;val&#x27;)\n    # find url=... (may be quoted or unquoted) and │
│ if the URL contains javascript:, replace whole URL with about:blank\n    def │
│ repl_url(match: re.Match) -> bytes:\n        pre = match.group(1)\n          │
│ urlval = match.group(2)\n        # strip optional quotes around url value\n  │
│ u = urlval.strip()\n        if                                               │
│ (u.lower().startswith(b&#x27;javascript:&#x27;)) or                          │
│ (b&#x27;javascript:&#x27; in u.lower()):\n            return pre +           │
│ b&#x27;about:blank&#x27;\n        return pre + urlval\n\n    val2 =          │
│ re.sub(br&#x27;(?i)(url\s=\s*)(["\&#x27;]?)([^;\s"]+)(["\&#x27;]?)&#x27;,    │
│ lambda mm:                                                                   │
│ repl_url(re.match(br&#x27;(url\s*=\s*)(["\&#x27;]?)([^;\s"]+)(["\&#x27;]?)&# │
│ x27;, mm.group(0))), val)\n    # fallback: if any javascript: remains,       │
│ replace it entirely\n    if b&#x27;javascript:&#x27; in val2.lower():\n      │
│ val2 = re.sub(br&#x27;(?i)javascript\s*:[^;\s]&#x27;,                        │
│ b&#x27;about:blank&#x27;, val2)\n    return prefix + q + val2 + q\n\ndata =  │
│ meta_content_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_meta(m), data)\n\n# 7) Final pass:   │
│ ensure remaining unquoted attributes beginning with javascript: are          │
│ replaced\nfinal_unquoted_js_re =                                             │
│ re.compile(br&#x27;(?P<prefix>\b(?:href|src|action|formaction)\s=\s*)(?P<val │
│ >javascript\s*:[^\s>]*)&#x27;, re_i)\n\ndef _final_unq(m: re.Match) ->       │
│ bytes:\n    return m.group(&#x27;prefix&#x27;) +                             │
│ b&#x27;about:blank&#x27;\n\ndata = final_unquoted_js_re.sub(lambda m:        │
│ _final_unq(m), data)\n\n# Write back only if changed\nif data != orig:\n     │
│ with open(path, &#x27;wb&#x27;) as f:\n                                      │
│ f.write(data)\n\nsys.exit(0)\nPY'}                                           │

│ ┃ 1) Remove <script ...>...</script> blocks (including attributes); handle ┃ │
│ ┃                            self-closing tags                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ script_re = re.compile(br'<script\b[^>]?(?:/>|>.?</script\s*>)', re_is) data │
│ = script_re.sub(b'', data)                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   2) Remove event handler attributes (on...=...), replace with single    ┃ │
│ ┃                          space to avoid merging                          ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ onattr_re =                                                                  │
│ re.compile(br'\s+on[a-z0-9_:-]+\s*=\s*(?:"[^"]"|&#x27;[^&#x27;]&#x27;|[^\s>] │
│ +)', re_i) data = onattr_re.sub(b' ', data)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                        3) URL-bearing attributes                         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ URL_ATTRS =                                                                  │
│ b'(?:href|src|formaction|xlink:href|background|action|cite|longdesc)'        │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                  quoted form: attr = "value" or 'value'                  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ url_attr_quoted_re = re.compile(br'(?P<prefix>\b' + URL_ATTRS +              │
│ br'\s*=\s*)(?P<quote>["&#x27;])(?P<val>.*?)(?P=quote)', re_is)               │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃       unquoted form: attr = value (value ends at whitespace or >)        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ url_attr_unquoted_re = re.compile(br'(?P<prefix>\b' + URL_ATTRS +            │
│ br'\s*=\s*)(?P<val>[^\s>]+)', re_i)                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ def sanitize_url_value(val: bytes) -> bytes: """Return sanitized value       │
│ (preserve if safe, otherwise return b'about:blank').""" low =                │
│ val.lstrip().lower() if low.startswith(b'javascript:') or b'javascript:' in  │
│ low: return b'about:blank' if low.startswith(b'data:') and (b'javascript' in │
│ low or b'vbscript' in low or (b'base64' in low and b'javascript' in low)):   │
│ return b'about:blank' return val                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ def _quoted_repl(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group('prefix') quote =   │
│ m.group('quote') val = m.group('val') newval = sanitize_url_value(val)       │
│ return prefix + quote + newval + quote                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ def _unquoted_repl(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group('prefix') val =   │
│ m.group('val') newval = sanitize_url_value(val) # keep it unquoted as        │
│ original return prefix + newval                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ data = url_attr_quoted_re.sub(lambda m: _quoted_repl(m), data) data =        │
│ url_attr_unquoted_re.sub(lambda m: _unquoted_repl(m), data)                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   4) Style attributes: remove expression(...) and replace javascript:    ┃ │
│ ┃                inside url(...) and any javascript: tokens                ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ style_re = re.compile(br'(\bstyle\s*=\s*)(["&#x27;])(?P<val>.?)(["&#x27;])', │
│ re_is) expr_re = re.compile(br'(?i)expression\s([^)])') url_js_re =          │
│ re.compile(br'(?i)url(\s["&#x27;]?\sjavascript\s:[^)&quot;&#x27;]["&#x27;]?\ │
│ s)') js_scheme_re = re.compile(br'(?i)javascript\s*:')                       │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_style(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q1 =          │
│ m.group(2) val = m.group('val') q2 = m.group(4) # remove old IE expression() │
│ usages v = expr_re.sub(b'', val) # neutralize any javascript: scheme         │
│ occurrences first v = js_scheme_re.sub(b'about:blank', v) # replace url(...) │
│ that previously contained javascript: with a safe url(about:blank) v =       │
│ url_js_re.sub(b'url(about:blank)', v) return prefix + q1 + v + q2            │
│                                                                              │
│ data = style_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_style(m), data)                      │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃       5) srcdoc attribute contains HTML; sanitize inside similarly       ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
