│ srcdoc_re =                                                                  │
│ re.compile(br'(\bsrcdoc\s*=\s*)(["&#x27;])(?P<val>.*?)(["&#x27;])', re_is)   │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_srcdoc(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q1 =         │
│ m.group(2) val = m.group('val') q2 = m.group(4) v = script_re.sub(b'', val)  │
│ v = onattr_re.sub(b' ', v) v = url_attr_quoted_re.sub(lambda mm:             │
│ _quoted_repl(mm), v) v = url_attr_unquoted_re.sub(lambda mm:                 │
│ _unquoted_repl(mm), v) v = style_re.sub(lambda mm: _sanitize_style(mm), v)   │
│ return prefix + q1 + v + q2                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ data = srcdoc_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_srcdoc(m), data)                    │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    6) meta refresh: replace url=... piece if it contains javascript:     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ meta_content_re =                                                            │
│ re.compile(br'(<meta\b[^>]\bcontent\s=\s*)(["&#x27;])(?P<val>.*?)(["&#x27;]) │
│ ', re_is)                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_meta(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q = m.group(2) │
│ val = m.group('val') # find url=... (may be quoted or unquoted) and if the   │
│ URL contains javascript:, replace whole URL with about:blank def             │
│ repl_url(match: re.Match) -> bytes: pre = match.group(1) urlval =            │
│ match.group(2) # strip optional quotes around url value u = urlval.strip()   │
│ if (u.lower().startswith(b'javascript:')) or (b'javascript:' in u.lower()):  │
│ return pre + b'about:blank' return pre + urlval                              │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  val2 =                                                                      │
│  re.sub(br&#x27;(?i)(url\s*=\s*)([&quot;\&#x27;]?)([^;\s&quot;]+)([&quot;\&  │
│  #x27;]?)&#x27;, lambda mm:                                                  │
│  repl_url(re.match(br&#x27;(url\s*=\s*)([&quot;\&#x27;]?)([^;\s&quot;]+)([&  │
│  quot;\&#x27;]?)&#x27;, mm.group(0))), val)                                  │
│  # fallback: if any javascript: remains, replace it entirely                 │
│  if b&#x27;javascript:&#x27; in val2.lower():                                │
│      val2 = re.sub(br&#x27;(?i)javascript\s*:[^;\s]*&#x27;,                  │
│  b&#x27;about:blank&#x27;, val2)                                             │
│  return prefix + q + val2 + q                                                │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ data = meta_content_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_meta(m), data)                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    7) Final pass: ensure remaining unquoted attributes beginning with    ┃ │
│ ┃                         javascript: are replaced                         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ final_unquoted_js_re =                                                       │
│ re.compile(br'(?P<prefix>\b(?:href|src|action|formaction)\s*=\s*)(?P<val>jav │
│ ascript\s*:[^\s>]*)', re_i)                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ def _final_unq(m: re.Match) -> bytes: return m.group('prefix') +             │
│ b'about:blank'                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ data = final_unquoted_js_re.sub(lambda m: _final_unq(m), data)               │

│ <output> #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ In-place HTML JavaScript sanitizer       │
│ (byte-oriented) that preserves formatting as much as possible.               │
│                                                                              │
│ This version fixes handling of unquoted attribute values, style url(...)     │
│ javascript:, and meta refresh url replacement by replacing the entire        │
│ dangerous attribute value or URL component with a safe token (about:blank)   │
│ rather than performing partial substitutions. """                            │
│                                                                              │
│ import sys import re                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ if len(sys.argv) != 2: print("Usage: filter.py <file.html>",                 │
│ file=sys.stderr) sys.exit(2)                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ path = sys.argv[1]                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ with open(path, 'rb') as f: data = f.read()                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ orig = data                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                                  flags                                   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ re_i = re.IGNORECASE re_is = re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL                       │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ 1) Remove <script ...>...</script> blocks (including attributes); handle ┃ │
│ ┃                            self-closing tags                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ script_re = re.compile(br'<script\b[^>]?(?:/>|>.?</script\s*>)', re_is) data │
│ = script_re.sub(b'', data)                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   2) Remove event handler attributes (on...=...), replace with single    ┃ │
│ ┃                          space to avoid merging                          ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ onattr_re =                                                                  │
│ re.compile(br'\s+on[a-z0-9_:-]+\s*=\s*(?:"[^"]"|&#x27;[^&#x27;]&#x27;|[^\s>] │
│ +)', re_i) data = onattr_re.sub(b' ', data)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                        3) URL-bearing attributes                         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ URL_ATTRS =                                                                  │
│ b'(?:href|src|formaction|xlink:href|background|action|cite|longdesc)'        │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                  quoted form: attr = "value" or 'value'                  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ url_attr_quoted_re = re.compile(br'(?P<prefix>\b' + URL_ATTRS +              │
│ br'\s*=\s*)(?P<quote>["&#x27;])(?P<val>.*?)(?P=quote)', re_is)               │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃       unquoted form: attr = value (value ends at whitespace or >)        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ url_attr_unquoted_re = re.compile(br'(?P<prefix>\b' + URL_ATTRS +            │
│ br'\s*=\s*)(?P<val>[^\s>]+)', re_i)                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ def sanitize_url_value(val: bytes) -> bytes: """Return sanitized value       │
│ (preserve if safe, otherwise return b'about:blank').""" low =                │
│ val.lstrip().lower() if low.startswith(b'javascript:') or b'javascript:' in  │
│ low: return b'about:blank' if low.startswith(b'data:') and (b'javascript' in │
│ low or b'vbscript' in low or (b'base64' in low and b'javascript' in low)):   │
│ return b'about:blank' return val                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ def _quoted_repl(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group('prefix') quote =   │
│ m.group('quote') val = m.group('val') newval = sanitize_url_value(val)       │
│ return prefix + quote + newval + quote                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ def _unquoted_repl(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group('prefix') val =   │
│ m.group('val') newval = sanitize_url_value(val) # keep it unquoted as        │
│ original return prefix + newval                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ data = url_attr_quoted_re.sub(lambda m: _quoted_repl(m), data) data =        │
│ url_attr_unquoted_re.sub(lambda m: _unquoted_repl(m), data)                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   4) Style attributes: remove expression(...) and replace javascript:    ┃ │
│ ┃                inside url(...) and any javascript: tokens                ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ style_re = re.compile(br'(\bstyle\s*=\s*)(["&#x27;])(?P<val>.?)(["&#x27;])', │
│ re_is) expr_re = re.compile(br'(?i)expression\s([^)])') url_js_re =          │
│ re.compile(br'(?i)url(\s["&#x27;]?\sjavascript\s:[^)&quot;&#x27;]["&#x27;]?\ │
│ s)') js_scheme_re = re.compile(br'(?i)javascript\s*:')                       │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_style(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q1 =          │
│ m.group(2) val = m.group('val') q2 = m.group(4) # remove old IE expression() │
│ usages v = expr_re.sub(b'', val) # neutralize any javascript: scheme         │
│ occurrences first v = js_scheme_re.sub(b'about:blank', v) # replace url(...) │
│ that previously contained javascript: with a safe url(about:blank) v =       │
│ url_js_re.sub(b'url(about:blank)', v) return prefix + q1 + v + q2            │
│                                                                              │
│ data = style_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_style(m), data)                      │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃       5) srcdoc attribute contains HTML; sanitize inside similarly       ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ srcdoc_re =                                                                  │
│ re.compile(br'(\bsrcdoc\s*=\s*)(["&#x27;])(?P<val>.*?)(["&#x27;])', re_is)   │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_srcdoc(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q1 =         │
│ m.group(2) val = m.group('val') q2 = m.group(4) v = script_re.sub(b'', val)  │
│ v = onattr_re.sub(b' ', v) v = url_attr_quoted_re.sub(lambda mm:             │
│ _quoted_repl(mm), v) v = url_attr_unquoted_re.sub(lambda mm:                 │
│ _unquoted_repl(mm), v) v = style_re.sub(lambda mm: _sanitize_style(mm), v)   │
│ return prefix + q1 + v + q2                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ data = srcdoc_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_srcdoc(m), data)                    │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    6) meta refresh: replace url=... piece if it contains javascript:     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ meta_content_re =                                                            │
│ re.compile(br'(<meta\b[^>]\bcontent\s=\s*)(["&#x27;])(?P<val>.*?)(["&#x27;]) │
│ ', re_is)                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_meta(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q = m.group(2) │
│ val = m.group('val') # find url=... (may be quoted or unquoted) and if the   │
│ URL contains javascript:, replace whole URL with about:blank def             │
│ repl_url(match: re.Match) -> bytes: pre = match.group(1) urlval =            │
│ match.group(2) # strip optional quotes around url value u = urlval.strip()   │
│ if (u.lower().startswith(b'javascript:')) or (b'javascript:' in u.lower()):  │
│ return pre + b'about:blank' return pre + urlval                              │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  val2 =                                                                      │
│  re.sub(br&#x27;(?i)(url\s*=\s*)([&quot;\&#x27;]?)([^;\s&quot;]+)([&quot;\&  │
│  #x27;]?)&#x27;, lambda mm:                                                  │
│  repl_url(re.match(br&#x27;(url\s*=\s*)([&quot;\&#x27;]?)([^;\s&quot;]+)([&  │
│  quot;\&#x27;]?)&#x27;, mm.group(0))), val)                                  │
│  # fallback: if any javascript: remains, replace it entirely                 │
│  if b&#x27;javascript:&#x27; in val2.lower():                                │
│      val2 = re.sub(br&#x27;(?i)javascript\s*:[^;\s]*&#x27;,                  │
│  b&#x27;about:blank&#x27;, val2)                                             │
│  return prefix + q + val2 + q                                                │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ data = meta_content_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_meta(m), data)                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    7) Final pass: ensure remaining unquoted attributes beginning with    ┃ │
│ ┃                         javascript: are replaced                         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ final_unquoted_js_re =                                                       │
│ re.compile(br'(?P<prefix>\b(?:href|src|action|formaction)\s*=\s*)(?P<val>jav │
│ ascript\s*:[^\s>]*)', re_i)                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ def _final_unq(m: re.Match) -> bytes: return m.group('prefix') +             │
│ b'about:blank'                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ data = final_unquoted_js_re.sub(lambda m: _final_unq(m), data)               │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                        Write back only if changed                        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if data != orig: with open(path, 'wb') as f: f.write(data)                   │
│                                                                              │
│ sys.exit(0) </output>                                                        │

│ url(&#x27;javascript:...&#x27;) with url(&#x27;about:blank&#x27;). In a few  │
│ quick runs the style attribute case showed the sanitizer logic working when  │
│ executed standalone; the script now contains the updated logic.\n\nNotes,    │
│ limitations, and caveats\n- This is a regex/byte-level sanitizer — not a     │
│ full HTML parser. It intentionally minimizes changes to the file, operating  │
│ on raw bytes and only modifying or removing dangerous substrings.\n- Some    │
│ minimal formatting differences are unavoidable (e.g., removing an attribute  │
│ leaves a single space to prevent merging attributes, and removing a script   │
│ block removes its bytes). The sanitizer attempts to keep those minimal.\n-   │
│ The sanitizer handles many common vectors (script tags, on* attributes,      │
│ javascript: and dangerous data: in attributes, style url(...) javascript,    │
│ srcdoc inner HTML, meta refresh), but it may not catch every obfuscated or   │
│ encoded vector. Defense-in-depth is recommended (CSP, proper output          │
│ encoding, input validation).\n- There may still be edge-cases with extremely │
│ malformed HTML or exotic encodings.\n\nFiles changed/created\n-              │

│     • Unquoted href/javascript: values were becoming about:blankalert(1)     │
│       (scheme replaced but payload left).                                    │
│     • javascript: inside style url(...) was not properly sanitized.          │
│     • meta refresh content produced about:blankalert(3).                     │

│     • Replace entire URL attribute values (both quoted and unquoted) with    │

│       trailing payload is not appended).                                     │
│     • Improve meta refresh handling to replace the url=... component with    │

│     • Implement a more robust style attribute sanitizer that inspects each   │
│       url(...) instance and, if the inner value contains javascript:,        │
│       replaces the inner value with about:blank while preserving quotes.     │

│    only modifying or removing dangerous substrings.                          │
│  • Some minimal formatting differences are unavoidable (e.g., removing an    │
│    attribute leaves a single space to prevent merging attributes, and        │
│    removing a script block removes its bytes). The sanitizer attempts to     │
│    keep those minimal.                                                       │
│  • The sanitizer handles many common vectors (script tags, on* attributes,   │
│    javascript: and dangerous data: in attributes, style url(...) javascript, │
│    srcdoc inner HTML, meta refresh), but it may not catch every obfuscated   │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                                  flags                                   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ re_i = re.IGNORECASE re_is = re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL                       │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ 1) Remove <script ...>...</script> blocks (including attributes); handle ┃ │
│ ┃                            self-closing tags                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ script_re = re.compile(br'<script\b[^>]?(?:/>|>.?</script\s*>)', re_is) data │
│ = script_re.sub(b'', data)                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   2) Remove event handler attributes (on...=...), replace with single    ┃ │
│ ┃                          space to avoid merging                          ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ onattr_re =                                                                  │
│ re.compile(br'\s+on[a-z0-9_:-]+\s*=\s*(?:"[^"]"|&#x27;[^&#x27;]&#x27;|[^\s>] │
│ +)', re_i) data = onattr_re.sub(b' ', data)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                        3) URL-bearing attributes                         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ URL_ATTRS =                                                                  │
│ b'(?:href|src|formaction|xlink:href|background|action|cite|longdesc)'        │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                  quoted form: attr = "value" or 'value'                  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ url_attr_quoted_re = re.compile(br'(?P<prefix>\b' + URL_ATTRS +              │
│ br'\s*=\s*)(?P<quote>["&#x27;])(?P<val>.*?)(?P=quote)', re_is)               │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃       unquoted form: attr = value (value ends at whitespace or >)        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ url_attr_unquoted_re = re.compile(br'(?P<prefix>\b' + URL_ATTRS +            │
│ br'\s*=\s*)(?P<val>[^\s>]+)', re_i)                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ def sanitize_url_value(val: bytes) -> bytes: """Return sanitized value       │
│ (preserve if safe, otherwise return b'about:blank').""" low =                │
│ val.lstrip().lower() if low.startswith(b'javascript:') or b'javascript:' in  │
│ low: return b'about:blank' if low.startswith(b'data:') and (b'javascript' in │
│ low or b'vbscript' in low or (b'base64' in low and b'javascript' in low)):   │
│ return b'about:blank' return val                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ def _quoted_repl(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group('prefix') quote =   │
│ m.group('quote') val = m.group('val') newval = sanitize_url_value(val)       │
│ return prefix + quote + newval + quote                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ def _unquoted_repl(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group('prefix') val =   │
│ m.group('val') newval = sanitize_url_value(val) # keep it unquoted as        │
│ original return prefix + newval                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ data = url_attr_quoted_re.sub(lambda m: _quoted_repl(m), data) data =        │
│ url_attr_unquoted_re.sub(lambda m: _unquoted_repl(m), data)                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   4) Style attributes: remove expression(...) and replace javascript:    ┃ │
│ ┃                             inside url(...)                              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ style_re = re.compile(rb'(\bstyle\s*=\s*)(["&#x27;])(?P<val>.?)(["&#x27;])', │
│ re_is) expr_re = re.compile(rb'(?i)expression\s([^)]*)')                     │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_style(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q1 =          │
│ m.group(2) val = m.group('val') q2 = m.group(4) # remove old IE expression() │
│ usages v = expr_re.sub(b'', val) # process url(...) occurrences: for each    │
│ url(...), if the inner value contains javascript:, # replace the inner value │
│ with about:blank preserving surrounding quotes/formatting out = bytearray()  │
│ i = 0 while True: murl = re.search(rb'(?i)url(', v[i:]) if not murl: out +=  │
│ v[i:] break start = i + murl.start() out += v[i:start] # parse after 'url('  │
│ j = start + murl.end() - murl.start() # skip whitespace while j < len(v) and │
│ v[j] in b' \t\n\r': j += 1 if j >= len(v): out += v[start:] break # check    │
│ for quote if v[j] in (ord("'"), ord('"')): q = v[j] content_start = j + 1 #  │
│ find closing quote k = content_start while k < len(v) and v[k] != q: k += 1  │
│ if k >= len(v): out += v[start:] break content = v[content_start:k] # find   │
│ closing parenthesis after the quote p = k + 1 while p < len(v) and v[p] in   │
│ b' \t\n\r': p += 1 if p >= len(v) or v[p] != ord(')'): out += v[start: p] i  │
│ = p continue if b'javascript:' in content.lower(): out += b'url(' +          │
│ bytes([q]) + b'about:blank' + bytes([q]) + b')' else: out += v[start:p+1] i  │
│ = p + 1 else: # unquoted content: read until closing ')' content_start = j k │
│ = content_start while k < len(v) and v[k] != ord(')'): k += 1 if k >=        │
│ len(v): out += v[start:] break content = v[content_start:k] if               │
│ b'javascript:' in content.lower(): out += b'url(about:blank)' else: out +=   │
│ v[start:k+1] i = k + 1 return prefix + q1 + bytes(out) + q2                  │
│                                                                              │
│ data = style_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_style(m), data)                      │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃       5) srcdoc attribute contains HTML; sanitize inside similarly       ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ srcdoc_re =                                                                  │
│ re.compile(br'(\bsrcdoc\s*=\s*)(["&#x27;])(?P<val>.*?)(["&#x27;])', re_is)   │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_srcdoc(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q1 =         │
│ m.group(2) val = m.group('val') q2 = m.group(4) v = script_re.sub(b'', val)  │
│ v = onattr_re.sub(b' ', v) v = url_attr_quoted_re.sub(lambda mm:             │
│ _quoted_repl(mm), v) v = url_attr_unquoted_re.sub(lambda mm:                 │
│ _unquoted_repl(mm), v) v = style_re.sub(lambda mm: _sanitize_style(mm), v)   │
│ return prefix + q1 + v + q2                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ data = srcdoc_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_srcdoc(m), data)                    │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    6) meta refresh: replace url=... piece if it contains javascript:     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ meta_content_re =                                                            │
│ re.compile(br'(<meta\b[^>]\bcontent\s=\s*)(["&#x27;])(?P<val>.*?)(["&#x27;]) │
│ ', re_is)                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_meta(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q = m.group(2) │
│ val = m.group('val') # find url=... (may be quoted or unquoted) and if the   │
│ URL contains javascript:, replace whole URL with about:blank def             │
│ repl_url(match: re.Match) -> bytes: pre = match.group(1) openq =             │
│ match.group(2) or b'' urlval = match.group(3) or b'' closeq = match.group(4) │
│ or b'' u = urlval.strip() if (u.lower().startswith(b'javascript:')) or       │
│ (b'javascript:' in u.lower()): # preserve original quoting if present return │
│ pre + openq + b'about:blank' + closeq return pre + openq + urlval + closeq   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  val2 =                                                                      │
│  re.sub(br&#x27;(?i)(url\s*=\s*)([&quot;\&#x27;]?)([^;\s&quot;]+)([&quot;\&  │
│  #x27;]?)&#x27;, lambda mm:                                                  │
│  repl_url(re.match(br&#x27;(url\s*=\s*)([&quot;\&#x27;]?)([^;\s&quot;]+)([&  │
│  quot;\&#x27;]?)&#x27;, mm.group(0))), val)                                  │
│  # fallback: if any javascript: remains, replace it entirely                 │
│  if b&#x27;javascript:&#x27; in val2.lower():                                │
│      val2 = re.sub(br&#x27;(?i)javascript\s*:[^;\s]*&#x27;,                  │
│  b&#x27;about:blank&#x27;, val2)                                             │
│  return prefix + q + val2 + q                                                │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ data = meta_content_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_meta(m), data)                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    7) Final pass: ensure remaining unquoted attributes beginning with    ┃ │
│ ┃                         javascript: are replaced                         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ final_unquoted_js_re =                                                       │
│ re.compile(br'(?P<prefix>\b(?:href|src|action|formaction)\s*=\s*)(?P<val>jav │
│ ascript\s*:[^\s>]*)', re_i)                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ def _final_unq(m: re.Match) -> bytes: return m.group('prefix') +             │
│ b'about:blank'                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ data = final_unquoted_js_re.sub(lambda m: _final_unq(m), data)               │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                        Write back only if changed                        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if data != orig: with open(path, 'wb') as f: f.write(data)                   │
│                                                                              │
│ sys.exit(0) </output>                                                        │

│                                                                              │
│ This version fixes handling of unquoted attribute values, style url(...)     │
│ javascript:, and meta refresh url replacement by replacing the entire        │
│ dangerous attribute value or URL component with a safe token (about:blank)   │
│ rather than performing partial substitutions. """                            │
│                                                                              │
│ import sys import re                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ if len(sys.argv) != 2: print("Usage: filter.py <file.html>",                 │
│ file=sys.stderr) sys.exit(2)                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ path = sys.argv[1]                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ with open(path, 'rb') as f: data = f.read()                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ orig = data                                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                                  flags                                   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │

│ re_i = re.IGNORECASE re_is = re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL                       │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ 1) Remove <script ...>...</script> blocks (including attributes); handle ┃ │
│ ┃                            self-closing tags                             ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ script_re = re.compile(br'<script\b[^>]?(?:/>|>.?</script\s*>)', re_is) data │
│ = script_re.sub(b'', data)                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   2) Remove event handler attributes (on...=...), replace with single    ┃ │
│ ┃                          space to avoid merging                          ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ onattr_re =                                                                  │
│ re.compile(br'\s+on[a-z0-9_:-]+\s*=\s*(?:"[^"]"|&#x27;[^&#x27;]&#x27;|[^\s>] │
│ +)', re_i) data = onattr_re.sub(b' ', data)                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                        3) URL-bearing attributes                         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ URL_ATTRS =                                                                  │
│ b'(?:href|src|formaction|xlink:href|background|action|cite|longdesc)'        │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                  quoted form: attr = "value" or 'value'                  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ url_attr_quoted_re = re.compile(br'(?P<prefix>\b' + URL_ATTRS +              │
│ br'\s*=\s*)(?P<quote>["&#x27;])(?P<val>.*?)(?P=quote)', re_is)               │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃       unquoted form: attr = value (value ends at whitespace or >)        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ url_attr_unquoted_re = re.compile(br'(?P<prefix>\b' + URL_ATTRS +            │
│ br'\s*=\s*)(?P<val>[^\s>]+)', re_i)                                          │
│                                                                              │
│ def sanitize_url_value(val: bytes) -> bytes: """Return sanitized value       │
│ (preserve if safe, otherwise return b'about:blank').""" low =                │
│ val.lstrip().lower() if low.startswith(b'javascript:') or b'javascript:' in  │
│ low: return b'about:blank' if low.startswith(b'data:') and (b'javascript' in │
│ low or b'vbscript' in low or (b'base64' in low and b'javascript' in low)):   │
│ return b'about:blank' return val                                             │
│                                                                              │
│ def _quoted_repl(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group('prefix') quote =   │
│ m.group('quote') val = m.group('val') newval = sanitize_url_value(val)       │
│ return prefix + quote + newval + quote                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ def _unquoted_repl(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group('prefix') val =   │
│ m.group('val') newval = sanitize_url_value(val) # keep it unquoted as        │
│ original return prefix + newval                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ data = url_attr_quoted_re.sub(lambda m: _quoted_repl(m), data) data =        │
│ url_attr_unquoted_re.sub(lambda m: _unquoted_repl(m), data)                  │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃   4) Style attributes: remove expression(...) and replace javascript:    ┃ │
│ ┃                             inside url(...)                              ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ style_re = re.compile(rb'(\bstyle\s*=\s*)(["&#x27;])(?P<val>.?)(["&#x27;])', │
│ re_is) expr_re = re.compile(rb'(?i)expression\s([^)]*)')                     │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_style(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q1 =          │
│ m.group(2) val = m.group('val') q2 = m.group(4) # remove old IE expression() │
│ usages v = expr_re.sub(b'', val) # process url(...) occurrences: for each    │
│ url(...), if the inner value contains javascript:, # replace the inner value │
│ with about:blank preserving surrounding quotes/formatting out = bytearray()  │
│ i = 0 while True: murl = re.search(rb'(?i)url(', v[i:]) if not murl: out +=  │
│ v[i:] break start = i + murl.start() out += v[i:start] # parse after 'url('  │
│ j = start + murl.end() - murl.start() # skip whitespace while j < len(v) and │
│ v[j] in b' \t\n\r': j += 1 if j >= len(v): out += v[start:] break # check    │
│ for quote if v[j] in (ord("'"), ord('"')): q = v[j] content_start = j + 1 #  │
│ find closing quote k = content_start while k < len(v) and v[k] != q: k += 1  │
│ if k >= len(v): out += v[start:] break content = v[content_start:k] # find   │
│ closing parenthesis after the quote p = k + 1 while p < len(v) and v[p] in   │
│ b' \t\n\r': p += 1 if p >= len(v) or v[p] != ord(')'): out += v[start: p] i  │
│ = p continue if b'javascript:' in content.lower(): out += b'url(' +          │
│ bytes([q]) + b'about:blank' + bytes([q]) + b')' else: out += v[start:p+1] i  │
│ = p + 1 else: # unquoted content: read until closing ')' content_start = j k │
│ = content_start while k < len(v) and v[k] != ord(')'): k += 1 if k >=        │
│ len(v): out += v[start:] break content = v[content_start:k] if               │
│ b'javascript:' in content.lower(): out += b'url(about:blank)' else: out +=   │
│ v[start:k+1] i = k + 1 return prefix + q1 + bytes(out) + q2                  │
│                                                                              │
│ data = style_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_style(m), data)                      │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃       5) srcdoc attribute contains HTML; sanitize inside similarly       ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ srcdoc_re =                                                                  │
│ re.compile(br'(\bsrcdoc\s*=\s*)(["&#x27;])(?P<val>.*?)(["&#x27;])', re_is)   │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_srcdoc(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q1 =         │
│ m.group(2) val = m.group('val') q2 = m.group(4) v = script_re.sub(b'', val)  │
│ v = onattr_re.sub(b' ', v) v = url_attr_quoted_re.sub(lambda mm:             │
│ _quoted_repl(mm), v) v = url_attr_unquoted_re.sub(lambda mm:                 │
│ _unquoted_repl(mm), v) v = style_re.sub(lambda mm: _sanitize_style(mm), v)   │
│ return prefix + q1 + v + q2                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ data = srcdoc_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_srcdoc(m), data)                    │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    6) meta refresh: replace url=... piece if it contains javascript:     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ meta_content_re =                                                            │
│ re.compile(br'(<meta\b[^>]\bcontent\s=\s*)(["&#x27;])(?P<val>.*?)(["&#x27;]) │
│ ', re_is)                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ def _sanitize_meta(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(1) q = m.group(2) │
│ val = m.group('val') # find url=... (may be quoted or unquoted) and if the   │
│ URL contains javascript:, replace whole URL with about:blank def             │
│ repl_url(match: re.Match) -> bytes: pre = match.group(1) openq =             │
│ match.group(2) or b'' urlval = match.group(3) or b'' closeq = match.group(4) │
│ or b'' u = urlval.strip() if (u.lower().startswith(b'javascript:')) or       │
│ (b'javascript:' in u.lower()): # preserve original quoting if present return │
│ pre + openq + b'about:blank' + closeq return pre + openq + urlval + closeq   │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│  val2 =                                                                      │
│  re.sub(br&#x27;(?i)(url\s*=\s*)([&quot;\&#x27;]?)([^;\s&quot;]+)([&quot;\&  │
│  #x27;]?)&#x27;, lambda mm:                                                  │
│  repl_url(re.match(br&#x27;(url\s*=\s*)([&quot;\&#x27;]?)([^;\s&quot;]+)([&  │
│  quot;\&#x27;]?)&#x27;, mm.group(0))), val)                                  │
│  # fallback: if any javascript: remains, replace it entirely                 │
│  if b&#x27;javascript:&#x27; in val2.lower():                                │
│      val2 = re.sub(br&#x27;(?i)javascript\s*:[^;\s]*&#x27;,                  │
│  b&#x27;about:blank&#x27;, val2)                                             │
│  return prefix + q + val2 + q                                                │
│                                                                              │
│                                                                              │
│ data = meta_content_re.sub(lambda m: _sanitize_meta(m), data)                │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    7) Final pass: ensure remaining unquoted attributes beginning with    ┃ │
│ ┃                         javascript: are replaced                         ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ final_unquoted_js_re =                                                       │
│ re.compile(br'(?P<prefix>\b(?:href|src|action|formaction)\s*=\s*)(?P<val>jav │
│ ascript\s*:[^\s>]*)', re_i)                                                  │
│                                                                              │
│ def _final_unq(m: re.Match) -> bytes: return m.group('prefix') +             │
│ b'about:blank'                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ data = final_unquoted_js_re.sub(lambda m: _final_unq(m), data)               │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                        Write back only if changed                        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ if data != orig: with open(path, 'wb') as f: f.write(data)                   │
│                                                                              │
│ sys.exit(0) </output>                                                        │

│ JavaScript XSS vectors\nwhile minimizing other changes.\n\nBehavior:\n-      │
│ Removes <script>...</script> blocks.\n- Strips on* event attributes          │
│ (onclick, onerror, ...).\n- Replaces javascript: and dangerous data: URIs in │
│ common URL attributes (quoted and unquoted)\n  by replacing the entire       │
│ attribute value with about:blank.\n- Sanitizes style attributes by replacing │
│ url(...) values that contain javascript: with about:blank.\n- Handles srcdoc │

│ fallbacks for url(javascript:...) occurrences.\n\nThis is a conservative     │

│ &#x27;rb&#x27;) as f:\n    data = f.read()\norig = data\n\n# Flags\nI =      │

│ Tool: Shell                                                                  │
│ Is error: False                                                              │
│ Content:                                                                     │
│ <exit-code> 0 </exit-code>                                                   │
│ <output> #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ Simpler byte-oriented HTML sanitizer     │
│ that removes common JavaScript XSS vectors while minimizing other changes.   │
│                                                                              │
│ Behavior:                                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│  • Removes <script>...</script> blocks.                                      │
│  • Strips on* event attributes (onclick, onerror, ...).                      │
│  • Replaces javascript: and dangerous data: URIs in common URL attributes    │
│    (quoted and unquoted) by replacing the entire attribute value with        │
│    about:blank.                                                              │
│  • Sanitizes style attributes by replacing url(...) values that contain      │
│    javascript: with about:blank.                                             │
│  • Handles srcdoc and meta refresh content attributes similarly.             │
│  • Performs a few global fallbacks for url(javascript:...) occurrences.      │
│                                                                              │
│ This is a conservative textual sanitizer (regex-based). It is not a full     │
│ HTML parser but attempts to avoid reserializing the document and preserve    │
│ formatting except where harmful substrings are removed or replaced. """      │
│                                                                              │
│ import sys import re                                                         │
│                                                                              │
│ if len(sys.argv) != 2: print("Usage: filter.py <file.html>",                 │
│ file=sys.stderr) sys.exit(2)                                                 │
│                                                                              │
│ path = sys.argv[1]                                                           │
│                                                                              │
│ with open(path, 'rb') as f: data = f.read() orig = data                      │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                                  Flags                                   ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ I = re.IGNORECASE IS = re.IGNORECASE | re.DOTALL                             │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃    Remove <script>...</script> blocks (including attributes), handle     ┃ │
│ ┃                               self-closing                               ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ script_re = re.compile(br"<script\b[^>]?(?:/>|>.?</script\s*>)", IS) data =  │
│ script_re.sub(b"", data)                                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                       Remove on* event attributes                        ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ onattr_re =                                                                  │
│ re.compile(br"\s+on[a-z0-9_:-]+\s*=\s*(?:&quot;[^&quot;]&quot;|'[^']'|[^\s>] │
│ +)", I) data = onattr_re.sub(b" ", data)                                     │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃              Helper: sanitize a URL value (bytes) -> bytes               ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ def sanitize_url_value(val: bytes) -> bytes: low = val.lstrip().lower() if   │
│ low.startswith(b"javascript:") or b"javascript:" in low: return              │
│ b"about:blank" if low.startswith(b"data:") and (b"javascript" in low or      │
│ b"vbscript" in low or (b"base64" in low and b"javascript" in low)): return   │
│ b"about:blank" return val                                                    │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                      Sanitize quoted URL attributes                      ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ url_quoted_re =                                                              │
│ re.compile(br"\b(href|src|formaction|action|background|cite|longdesc)\s*=\s* │
│ ([&quot;'])(?P<val>.*?)(\2)", IS)                                            │
│                                                                              │
│ def repl_url_quoted(m: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix = m.group(0) #             │
│ reconstruct: attr=<quote>val<quote> attr = m.group(1) quote = m.group(2) val │
│ = m.group('val') new = sanitize_url_value(val) return attr.encode('ascii') + │
│ b"=" + quote + new + quote                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ data = url_quoted_re.sub(lambda m: repl_url_quoted(m), data)                 │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃                     Sanitize unquoted URL attributes                     ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ url_unquoted_re =                                                            │
│ re.compile(br"\b(href|src|formaction|action|background|cite|longdesc)\s*=\s* │
│ ([^\s>]+)", I)                                                               │
│                                                                              │
│ def repl_url_unquoted(m: re.Match) -> bytes: attr = m.group(1) val =         │
│ m.group(2) new = sanitize_url_value(val) return attr.encode('ascii') + b"="  │
│ + new                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ data = url_unquoted_re.sub(lambda m: repl_url_unquoted(m), data)             │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃ Sanitize style attributes: replace url(...) occurrences with javascript: ┃ │
│ ┃                                  inside                                  ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ style_re = re.compile(br"\bstyle\s*=\s*([&quot;'])(?P<val>.?)(\1)", IS)      │
│ url_js_in_style =                                                            │
│ re.compile(br"url(\s(['&quot;])?\sjavascript\s:[^)]\1?\s)", I)               │
│                                                                              │
│ def repl_style(m: re.Match) -> bytes: quote = m.group(1) val =               │
│ m.group('val') v = val out = bytearray() pos = 0 L = len(v) low = v.lower()  │
│ while True: idx = low.find(b'url(', pos) if idx == -1: out += v[pos:] break  │
│ out += v[pos:idx] j = idx + 4 # skip whitespace while j < L and v[j] in b'   │
│                                                                              │
│ ': j += 1 if j >= L: out += v[idx:] break if v[j] in (ord("'"), ord('"')): q │
│ = v[j] content_start = j+1 k = v.find(bytes([q]), content_start) if k == -1: │
│ out += v[idx:] break content = v[content_start:k] p = k+1 while p < L and    │
│ v[p] in b'                                                                   │
│                                                                              │
│ ': p += 1 if p < L and v[p] == ord(')'): # complete url(...) if              │
│ content.lstrip().lower().startswith(b'javascript:'): out +=                  │
│ b'url(about:blank)' else: out += v[idx:p+1] pos = p+1 continue else: out +=  │
│ v[idx:] break else: # unquoted k = v.find(b')', j) if k == -1: out +=        │
│ v[idx:] break content = v[j:k] if                                            │
│ content.lstrip().lower().startswith(b'javascript:'): out +=                  │
│ b'url(about:blank)' else: out += v[idx:k+1] pos = k+1 continue return        │
│ b'style=' + quote + bytes(out) + quote                                       │
│                                                                              │
│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃     Global fallbacks: url('javascript:...'), url("javascript:..."),      ┃ │
│ ┃                           url(javascript:...)                            ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ data = re.sub(br"url(\s*'\sjavascript\s:[^)])", b"url('about:blank')", data, │
│ flags=I) data = re.sub(br'url(\s"\sjavascript\s:[^)])',                      │
│ b'url("about:blank")', data, flags=I) data =                                 │
│ re.sub(br"url(\sjavascript\s*:[^)]*)", b"url(about:blank)", data, flags=I)   │

│ ┏━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┓ │
│ ┃      srcdoc: sanitize inner HTML (remove scripts and on* and unsafe      ┃ │
│ ┃                               URLs/styles)                               ┃ │
│ ┗━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━┛ │
│                                                                              │
│ srcdoc_re = re.compile(br"\bsrcdoc\s*=\s*([&quot;'])(?P<val>.*?)(\1)", IS)   │
│                                                                              │
│ def repl_srcdoc(m: re.Match) -> bytes: quote = m.group(1) val =              │
│ m.group('val') v = script_re.sub(b"", val) v = onattr_re.sub(b" ", v) v =    │
│ url_quoted_re.sub(lambda mm: repl_url_quoted(mm), v) v =                     │
│ url_unquoted_re.sub(lambda mm: repl_url_unquoted(mm), v) v =                 │
│ style_re.sub(lambda mm: repl_style(mm), v) return b"srcdoc=" + quote + v +   │
│ quote                                                                        │
│                                                                              │
│ data = srcdoc_re.sub(lambda m: repl_srcdoc(m), data)                         │
│                                                                              │

│ ┃                         inside content attribute                         ┃ │

│ meta_re =                                                                    │
│ re.compile(br"<meta\b[^>]\bcontent\s=\s*([&quot;'])(?P<val>.*?)(\1)", IS)    │
│                                                                              │
│ def repl_meta(m: re.Match) -> bytes: quote = m.group(1) val = m.group('val') │
│ # replace url=... parts def repl_inner(mm: re.Match) -> bytes: prefix =      │
│ mm.group(1) urlval = mm.group(3) if b'javascript:' in urlval.lower(): return │

│  • This implementation attempts to avoid accidental quote mismatches and     │
│    malformed attributes by replacing entire attribute values (not just       │
│    schemes) when unsafe patterns are detected.                               │
│  • It is still possible to craft obfuscated/encoded variants that this       │
│    textual approach may miss (e.g., HTML-encoded javascript: tokens,         │
│    uncommon MIME schemes, or clever chaining). For critical applications     │
│    consider using a proper HTML parser + sanitizer and defense-in-depth      │
│    (CSP, output encoding).                                                   │
│                                                                              │
